#+title: lighthouseless — one runtime, one datamap, a fixed schema, a repository per daemon, traits as the domain's word, Note as the one semantic row
#+filetags: :runtime:paladin:typology:instance:lighthouse:datamap:traits:note:registry:ledger:

The tangle below is PRODUCT code only, regenerated from the rig against the repo and the published registry: 178 files (38 new whole, 129 edits as diffs, 11 moved as they stand), 23 moves and 37 dying; the test files are the 109 rows of =* tests — land from the captured rig=. Replayed through =methods/apply-diffs.py= on a fresh copy of the repo: moves first, 0 misses, the 243 product paths byte-equal to the rig (178) or absent on both (65). Census (=HOME= empty): typology 136·0 · paladin 48·0 · ghost 104·1 · runtime 71·7 · anima 78·2 · education 40·0 · chess 25·0 — every red attributed (pre-existing · m78 · five runtime files reading the published =@education= through =accio=, 79·1 once the registry is tapped). Known-issue 582 green through =Twitch=. Every campaign-2 fork ruled; =v.cast= copies before it fills, PROVEN 10-08 with the domains back on =export * as=; the runtime entities flat, =assemble.js= in the belt (his mark, 10-08). Open for beef: m78's order; then ONE landing, then the ledger by hand.
* intent

beef, verbatim — each strand lettered; every later row cites its letters.

| # | strand |
|---+--------|
| A | /"instances … Right now they're .viva.js, and I want them to be probably just JSON"/ |
| B | /"each daemon has its own turns and buffers, and all of the user space is per daemon. I want to pull that on to the runtime level"/ |
| C | /"pull the whole lighthouse into the runtime … one data map or one database micro instance for the entire runtime, where we have the user space, terminals, turns, buffers, threads"/ |
| D | /"We have one identity, one user. … they merge. It's one"/ |
| E | /"daemon level custom entities … the trace, and … review, formerly memory, they will need to be recast"/ → /"STI, custom traits, new nodes entity … It's not the most incredible, but it gets the job done and works. Fuck it."/ · /"it's note with a T"/ |
| F | /"only one lighthouse per runtime. … It stops being a service and it's just a property of the runtime. … I think we can still call it a lighthouse"/ |
| G | /"should greatly simplify the clients as well, but that is … a concern for later. The clients are pretty shit at the moment anyways"/ |
| H | /"we still want to move instances around and preset them and copy them … when the runtime boots, the instance gets pulled into the runtime. We do a diff. If anything is missing, we install it. We still do the whole registry pulling stuff from the Paladin"/ |
| I | /"a watch mechanism, Twitch, a subscriber … whenever the user affects a change in the composition of daemons and modes, we persist that to the instance. So the instance is kind of like a loader and a representation"/ |
| J | /"the runtime … greater role … with the registry. … the runtime to publish the registry … those are the modes that are being loaded, those are the packages … the runtime to be able to create and pull and do all of that … paladin is still the thing that owns the disk … paladin still owns instance"/ |
| K | /"all of the literals and stuff need to get a relationship with a daemon, so that we know what literals belong into which daemon"/ |
| L | /"Do I want a Cortex on runtime level? Probably. … hallucinators once on … the instance level or the runtime level or the daemon level, … or maybe even on the mode level … Maybe that's out of scope for now … Maybe we don't even want that"/ |
| M | /"the home. So when you open the client, you don't go directly into a daemon, you just start on runtime level … you're capable of creating turns outside of a daemon"/ · /"No home daemon, the runtime manages itself"/ |
| N | /"We don't no longer need all of this enroll shebang. … much more classical user management"/ |
| O | /"the prune, it's fine. We don't even need the .bug backup"/ |
| P | /"the thunks is for environment variable loading, and there we can just use whatever is the default … Inline modules, we might still want to do them via path"/ |
| Q | /"If a daemon returns a twitch, we can add a filter in front of the twitch … It's just a middleware in the end"/ |
| R | /"the slug is unique per daemon, that will still hold"/ |
| S | /"Thread and terminal? … the terminal is a kind of almost ephemeral entity, and the thread is a persistent entity"/ |
| T | /"not all databases support attach"/ · /"that seems like the wrong approach"/ · /"We don't need a duplication here"/ (discriminator = daemon) |
| U | /"I think just body, state, salience, due, and audience all go into the traits eventually"/ · /"Just a labeled description, yes"/ · /"the node should not have a required daemon … Node required on the Slack, sure … the daemon nullable, which is fine. And the absence of daemon … from runtime"/ |
| V | /"Paladin owns the disk. The runtime reads the instance at boot and writes back through the Paladin. Rows win while running, JSON wins at boot."/ |
| W | /"I don't think we need to restart on a new daemon necessarily. … it would probably be kind of quick. It's just a zip zip. … Not sure yet."/ |
| X | /"Stop using the word doors. … We don't have drivers or doors anywhere."/ — the vocabulary of this quest: an aperture OPENS a route; education's retention code is education's, never a runtime noun |
| Y | /"I want to use this opportunity to migrate to Micro 7. So whatever solution we pick has to be compatible with Micro 7."/ · /"TPT instead of STI (mikro 7)"/ · /"what is the specific case when we have to restart?"/ |
| Z | the review rulings, 10-07 — /"We will not port data, we will just drop what is existing … the databases are all trash. We start from scratch. This also means a lot of work on the ledger itself. This is a big migration."/ · /"call it not entry, but mask"/ · /"I don't want the daemon in the name, so it's Italian literal"/ · /"a single dedicated subscriber to fire or capture the event, and then we pass that into the Twitch … where we now define subscribers in, for example, the domain, we will do that via Twitch from now on"/ · /"not all modes know what daemon they are in. So we need to have the generic after literal create. This is paramount."/ · /"Do we really need STI? … we might be able to get away with just trait-based customization. So we wouldn't have to fuck with schema.update and shit at all. That would clarify the customization into two directions. We have the repository and then we have traits."/ · /"I don't want to authenticate at the root … we're gonna protect branches just like we already do"/ · /"Anima is within scope of this … it is something that we will think about later"/ · /"That is a known issue. We can't have write back on watched modes."/ |

* forks — beef's, open

1. *Note uniqueness* — RULED /"1. b."/: index-level. Not eight partial indexes (one per null pattern) — one expression unique index over =coalesce()= of the three nullable keys plus =slug=, declared in =NoteSchema.uniques= with =expression=; =schema.create= emits it, a duplicate =(null, null, null, "preset.meet")= throws =UNIQUE constraint failed: index 'Note_scope_slug_unique'=, =(null, dojo, null, "x")= beside =(null, null, null, "x")= is accepted, diff after create empty (=v7/note.unique.v7.js=, 7.2.3 file db). REJECTED: (a) =ensure()= as the only write path (a =create= races past it) · (c) a sentinel row.
2. *=mode= on Literal / Symbol, and the unique* — RULED beef 10-07: /"So I still want the literals and symbols to be unique per daemon. I don't need them to be unique per mode. They may require a mode, but I don't think we want uniqueness on either of those beyond slug and daemon."/ — =mode= REQUIRED, unique =(daemon, slug)=. A module mounted twice in one daemon shares the row: the second install finds it by slug through the daemon's repository and patches it; the row keeps the mode that installed it first. REJECTED: =(daemon, mode, slug)= (a, what the tangle first wrote) · =mode= nullable (b).
3. *the merged row's name* — RULED /"3 user"/: =User=. Terminal's =identity= FK becomes =user=; the lighthouse's =identity= routes become =user=.
4. *a new daemon while running* (W Y) — RESOLVED, beef's /"Isn't that just a create?"/: yes, and with STI gone the ORM is not even consulted. NO ROUTE does it yet (reading 10-07: /"we dont yet add any of the implementation to have the runtime add list remove or anything to daemons. none."/) — today it is the file and a restart; the shape below is what a later route would do. Adding a daemon at runtime = Daemon row · =mint(slug, traits)= → its repositories · =processes.daemon.create → execute= (the boot's own calls). A changed trait set = re-mint that daemon's repositories. Nothing needs a restart but env/statics changes (today's rule). REJECTED: "the entity set is frozen at init" — false even under STI (=discoverEntity(hierarchy, reset)=, measured), and moot without it.
5. *cortex* (L) — RULED /"once per runtime, and then the daemon can extend the set. So the ones that the daemon provides are on top, en plus."/: =die.runtime.cortex= is built once from =instance.hallucinators=; a daemon's cortex is =die.runtime.cortex.split(faculties)= (a copy) with the daemon's own =hallucinators= registered on top — never a replacement, never a filter. The mode rung deferred.
6. *=strength= is computed, not stored* — RULED beef 10-07: /"if we can pull off SQL formula inside of the traits, that would be fantastic. So if, then c."/ — (c), measured: the trait's =byStrength= orders on a =raw()= CASE over =json_extract(trait, '$.RETAINED.…')= built from the drivers' own =sql.strength=; =exp()= exists on better-sqlite3 (m78's driver, /"better-sqlite3."/), NOT on Deno's node:sqlite (=no such function: exp=) — =v7/strength.v7.js=: =stale < streak < mid < fresh < learnt=, =strength < 0.5 → stale=. =strength= is never stored; no index (it depends on now). REJECTED: (a) stored + a sweep · (b) sorted in JS.
* forks — campaign 1, open (beef's; numbered on from fork 6)

7. *which tier mints and signs the jwt* (=two-tiers-mint-the-jwt=, m0) — context: the runtime signs every user's tokens with =SECRET_VIVA_JWT=; on the rig =viva ledger/init= AND =viva instance/init= each mint a fresh secret when the key is blank, so the instance's =.env= shadows the ledger's and the two files hold two different secrets (measured, m0 LOG). Code before: =systems/ghost/belt/envfile.js= =MINTED = { SECRET_VIVA_JWT }= read by both verbs (the PROVEN block below). Code after: one verb mints. The choice in one line: the secret is the INSTANCE's (its datamap holds its users; a token from instance A can never verify a user of instance B) — =instance/init= mints, =ledger/init= never does, =ledger.json= still DECLARES the key so a machine-wide value can be set by hand.

| option | who mints | what the ledger's =.env= holds | migrate |
|--------+-----------+--------------------------------+---------|
| a | =ledger/init= only; instances inherit | the one secret | every instance shares it |
| b (lean) | =instance/init= only | the key, blank | per instance, first init |
| c (the rig) | both | a second secret the instance shadows | none |

8. *what a thread with no mode DOES* (=turn-over-userspace-undesigned=, m2) — strand M: /"you're capable of creating turns outside of a daemon"/ · /"No home daemon, the runtime manages itself"/. The mechanism half is ruled below (a turn lands on a runtime-level thread over =/userspace/entities/turn/create=; the 409 was =scoped("data")= minting a second Thread). The product half is his: a runtime-level thread holds turns the user writes — who answers them? Code before: nothing answers; a turn is a stored message. Code after: (a) nothing — the home is a notebook, turns are the user's own; (b) the runtime's cortex answers through a runtime-level harness (the cortex ladder's runtime rung, strand L, fork 5 built the cortex once per runtime); (c) the thread is a launcher only — a turn is refused unless the thread has a mode. The choice in one line: (a) now — the runtime stores, nothing answers, no harness and no =/cortex= route at the runtime until a mode-less answer is a product (deliverable 11 draws the home as threads · terminals · notes).

| option | who answers a runtime-level turn | needs |
|--------+----------------------------------+-------|
| a (lean) | nobody | nothing |
| b | the runtime's cortex | a runtime harness · a protected =/userspace/cortex= |
| c | refused | a 4xx on =turn/create= when =thread.mode= is null |
* the law

- one schema, one table per kind, FIXED at the kernel; a daemon is a COLUMN and a REPOSITORY, never a class, a file, a namespace or a second ORM. The schema's only authority is the libsql =Migrator=; nothing calls =schema.update= at runtime. REJECTED 10-07: STI / TPT (strand Z).
- customisation has two directions and no third: the REPOSITORY (per daemon, per kind) and the TRAIT. A trait has three halves — =schematic= (the payload under =row.trait[NAME]=), =repository= (verbs over the table, =this= the minted repository, per daemon), =entity= (METHODS on the entity CLASS — the old =methods= slot back under its new name: =word.assess()=, =this= the row; gated by the claim, since one class serves every daemon; =collide= runs across every mounted daemon's traits at boot) — /"re-add methods as entity"/. A trait never adds a column (the schema is FIXED); a sort over a payload field is =json_extract=.
- a minted repository scopes =daemon= on EVERY verb through mikro's own filter (=filters: { daemon: { daemon: slug } }= on each call; the =daemon= filter declared =default: false= on Literal · Symbol · Mode · Note), stamps =daemon= on =create · upsert · unique=, and reaches its siblings as =this.entities.<kind>= and its daemon as =this.daemon=; a =where= is never spread. The kernel repository (=em.getRepository(Literal)=, =datamap.entities.literal=) sees every daemon. Measured =probes/scope.js=.
- a domain brings traits, schematics, aperture routes, tools, a twitch — never a table, never a class, never a subscriber, never a =resolve= hook over its own routes. Education's retention code (bayesian · boolean · counter) is education's and stays there; each driver carries its strength formula in two dialects, =strength(held, now)= and =sql.strength(at)=.
- a row has a trait by CLAIM (=traits[]=); the gate is =traits.claim(row, name, vocabulary)= in the typology belt — the payload's defaults FILLED on a clone, then judged — reached as =repository.claim(row, name)= on a minted repository and as =v.<kind>.trait(row, name)= on a client descriptor.
- =fill= and =cast= (=v.fill= · =v.cast= · a schematic's own) RETURN the filled value and never write their argument: the plain objects and module namespaces along the schematic are copied first (=fresh=, =subsystems/typology/schematics/v.js:37=), then typebox's =Default= fills the copy; a schematic and a class instance are atomic, as typebox's own =Clone= keeps them — a class instance is filled where it stands (paladin's =Instance= in =settle=). Every caller takes the return. A domain keeps =export * as traits= · =export * as schematics=. REJECTED 10-08: plain-object domains (campaign 2's stub — every module would learn the cast's habit) · a whole =Value.Clone= before the fill (it flattens a schematic's Proxy: =schematic.fill= is undefined on the copy, =~/.viva/bak/ikiro/m80-campaign-20261007/cast/probes/wholeclone.probe.js=).
- the instance file is paladin's: the authored file is an =InstanceMask= (references, no seats), the settled unit an =Instance=; the runtime reads it at boot, diffs against its rows, installs what is missing, prunes on =--prune=, and writes nothing back — =Daemon.mask= is the RAW entry, a =${KEY}= never expands into a row or the file. JSON wins at boot; there is no running change. (V Z, writeback cut on reading 10-07)
- no runtime route touches composition or the registry: =instance.json= and the ledger verbs are the only way in. There is no =/registry=, no =/instance=, no =/metadata/registry=. (J, cut on reading 10-07)
- the lighthouse authenticates on the protected BRANCHES (=/daemon/*= · =/userspace=), never the root; =ctx.user= is bound behind each; =/attached/*= · =/metadata= · =/status= · =/lighthouse/*= are open; no route verifies a token by calling itself; the daemon's own =/cortex= is protected under its branch and the runtime has none. (F N Z)
- the twitch has ONE place: =datamap.registerSubscriber(twitch)= (one argument) builds the subscriber; the daemon is BOUND on its own twitch — =new Twitch().use(shard.context.bind("daemon", die.daemon)).use(shard.datamap.owned())= (=systems/runtime/lifecycle/daemon/population.js:29=), =owned()= drops a foreign daemon's rows, =ctx.daemon= is the FULL live daemon; the subscriber runs every =/after= hearing detached and inside a fresh scope forked off the event's em, and leaves =/before= hearings on the event (they mutate the row). The runtime's twitch carries no bind and no =owned()=. A twitch is a =Twitch=: =open= and =graft= COMPOSE at an occupied path — many ears, one event. A domain or a mode hears the KIND's name — =/after/literal/create= — never a daemon's. (Q Z; ruled (b) on campaign 1, /"i am not dogmatic about 1 sub"/ · /"twiches are rich."/) Measured =probes/subscriber.js=.
- the domain is a trait SOURCE like any kernel entry, never a class, a layer or a table. /"The domain does not play in here. The domain might configure stuff, but so might any other mode."/
- =traits= on Literal · Symbol · Note · User · Terminal is a JSON string array; the vocabulary is the daemon's composed trait set; the ORM enum stays on Mode (the runtime's catalog) and Buffer.
- the runtime's own kinds: a thread · turn · buffer · activity with no mode is the runtime's own (=mode= nullable); a note with no daemon is the runtime's (=daemon= nullable, the runtime mints a =note= repository only — Literal · Symbol · Mode carry =daemon= REQUIRED, a null-scoped repository of them could never find a row).
- =/userspace/entities/note= serves the KERNEL note repository (a user's notes span daemons; the =user= filter keeps them the user's own or shared) over the runtime twitch; no daemon grafts a second note route.
- no data crosses: every db on every shelf is dropped; a user signs up again. (Z)

A retention = Note ={ user, literal, traits: ["RETAINED"], trait.RETAINED: { driver, status, type, lastSignal, lastAt, due, state } }= read through the trait's entity half as ={ status, lastSignal, due, strength, is: { virgin, weak, familiar, strong, succeeded, failed } }=; a trace = Note ={ user, literal, parent: <retention>, traits: ["TRACED"], trait.TRACED: { signal, snapshot } }= (chess: =TRACED: { kind, thread, signal, status, snapshot }= — one name, two payloads, legal per daemon: the vocabulary is the daemon's); a dojo preset = Note ={ mode: dojo, slug: "preset.meet", traits: ["CONFIG", "LABELED"] }=; a runtime-level note = Note ={ daemon: null, mode: null, … }=. =due= is a stored ISO path; =strength= is a formula over the payload and now in two dialects — never stored, never indexed (fork 6c, =strength.v7.js=; the JS dialect pinned against the SQL one by =literal.test.js=).

* the ledger migration — by hand, the checklist (the scout of 10-07)

| where | finding | M |
|-------+---------+---|
| =~/.viva/.env= | the three lighthouse keys live HERE (=VIVA_LIGHTHOUSE_MOUNT= · =PUBLIC_VIVA_LIGHTHOUSE_REMOTE= · =SECRET_VIVA_JWT=), not in =~/.config/viva/env=; commented out by hand (a =.env= is authored, unset = commented) | 0 |
| =~/.viva/instances/lighthouse/= | the shared lighthouse mountpoint (not in =instances.json=): =multiplayer.viva.db= + migrations — wiped whole | 0 |
| =~/.viva/instances/*/mountpoint/service_multiplayer/= ×9 | every shelf ALSO carries its own =multiplayer.viva.db= (+ =tokens.json= in 6), two schema generations — the mountpoint wipe takes them | 0 |
| =instances.json= | =italian= row dangles (no shelf); =stucatch= · =lighthouse= shelves unregistered — migrate drops the dangling row, touches only registered shelves, wipes =lighthouse= by name | 0 |
| =instances/language-learning/= | NO =instance.viva.js=: the recipe is =test.viva.js= (depth-0 rule), daemons in =education.js= (three, =consume.nlp= thunks + =new Url= at =:38,40,80,82,124,126=), =development.js= orphaned (per-daemon =hallucinators= thunk =:33=); only italian boots; spanish 6.8 MB · brazilian 29 MB · playground · standalone dbs stale | 0 |
| =instances/{chess,strata}/daemon.js:30-33= · =education.js= | =consume.*.statics= hold thunks and =new Url(...)= — JSON carries STRINGS; the consumer constructs the Url (ruled below) | 0 |
| =instances/hello-world/= | kernel =./mode.viva.js= shelf-RELATIVE (a path, allowed) · =aperture.js= orphaned · stray 0-byte =hello.viva.db= at the root · =mode.viva.js= · =harness.js= are modules, not recipe | 0 |
| =instances/vivalence/instance.viva.js:5= | slug =vcompany= ≠ shelf =vivalence=; =daemon.js= differs from the registry's shipped copy | 0 |
| =registry/{droneaid,media,vcompany}/instances/*= · =~/code/strata/instances/strata/*= | four packages SHIP =instances/<slug>/= recipes — the migrator converts those too (=registry/tap= copies them); strata's shipped recipe still declares =lighthouse= · =services= · a JWT function default =:48= | 0 |
| =registry/stucatch/vivaware= | manifest at =vivaware/package.viva.js=, not the root; shelf =environment.json= already plain JSON (=:5= names the lighthouse remote) | 0 |
| =ledger.viva.js:35-47,94-113= | =services= (lighthouse) · =lighthouse= slot · the JWT function default — all die; =runtime · datamap · hallucinators · clients · environment= carry thunks at =:15,16,25,30,52= | 0 |
| =sessions/932= | stale (pid dead) — =ledger/doctor= reaps it | — |
| =bak/= 527 MB · =logs/= 432 KB | untouched; =bak/= takes each shelf's capture before the wipe (skill =readme=) | 0 |

- =consume.*.statics= in JSON are strings; a consumer that needs a =Url= constructs it (=new Url(statics.remote)=) — the recipe never carries an object with a prototype.
- the migration is BY HAND (beef, reading 10-07): every recipe =paladin.belt.find.viva= finds on a REGISTERED shelf (depth-0 rule — =test.viva.js= is language-learning's), plus each tapped package's shipped =instances/<slug>/= and =commons/instances/*=; unregistered shelves (=stucatch=) are left; =instances/lighthouse= is wiped by name; the dangling =italian= row is dropped; the tables above are the checklist.
- =Service= stays (=nlp-stanza= · =stockfish=); only the lighthouse SERVICE entry and the DATAMAP trait die — the ATTACHED trait and =/attached/process/service/<slug>= survive.
- the userspace kinds in a daemon's bag (=buffer · thread · turn · intent · activity=) are the RUNTIME's repositories handed through — unscoped by daemon, =user=-filtered; =ctx.daemon.entities.buffer= keeps working, which is why the 67 =entities.buffer= sites are not on the table.
- education's =literal_uses= insert moves from raw SQL to the repository verb that owns =uses=; =dojo='s =keepQuietly= raw Buffer update becomes =nativeUpdate=.
* rulings — design pass, 10-07

Every unknown of campaign 1 has exactly one fate: =RULED= (mine, the code is in the tangle), =RULED (beef)= (his words in =* decisions=, the shape mine), =FORK 7= · =FORK 8= (his, =* forks — campaign 1=), =m78= (a v7 line in m78's =* handoff=). Grouped by MECHANISM; a probe is a file under =scratchpad/design/probes/=, run from the rig root with =deno run -A --no-check --config deno.jsonc ../design/probes/<file>= on mikro 6.6.7.

| slug | mechanism | ruling | probe |
|------+-----------+--------+-------|
| mint-scope-holes | scope | RULED — the filter on every verb | scope.js |
| mint-where-not-object | scope | RULED — the where is never spread | scope.js |
| datarepository-count-skips-traits | scope | RULED — =count= · =findAndCount= · =findOneOrFail= resolve claims | scope.js |
| mint-repository-entity-class | scope | RULED — the NAME, in =strike= | scope.js |
| trait-verb-reaches-sibling-repository | scope | RULED — =this.entities= · =this.daemon= | — |
| daemon-twitch-branch-unreachable | twitch | RULED (beef) — (b), the subscriber in one place | subscriber.js |
| domain-twitch-has-no-daemon | twitch | RULED (beef) — rich: =ctx.daemon= + a fresh scope | subscriber.js |
| twitch-export-ambiguous | twitch | RULED — module-private, the descriptor's =.twitch= | — |
| twitch-fold-into-runtime | twitch | RULED — =Twitch= composes at an occupied path | subscriber.js |
| fresh-literal-fold-on-create | twitch | RULED — as the subscriber did; the gate says update | m1 fold.js |
| dataset-clears-the-request-context | twitch | RULED — unchanged; c2 measures a trait holding a row across DATASET | — |
| retention-reads-have-no-successor | retention | RULED (beef) — the RETAINED trait's =entity: { assess }= | — |

** the scope — a filter on every verb, never a spread where

Measured (=probes/scope.js=, 6.6.7): a minted repository that declares a =daemon= FILTER on the schema (=default: false=) and enables it on EVERY verb through mikro's own =options.filters= scopes =find · findOne(<id string>) · findOneOrFail · findAll · findAndCount · count · nativeUpdate · nativeDelete= — and so =updateOne · update · removeOne · remove · ensure= that ride them — while the kernel repository (=em.find(Literal, …)=, =em.getRepository(Literal)=) stays unscoped (2 rows of =casa=) and a =populate= under the filter joins. A =where= naming another daemon ANDs with the filter → =[]= (the scope wins, silently — the one case a caller must not rely on, =COVERAGE GAP=). The leg's six-verb override leaked on =findOneOrFail · findAll · updateOne= and broke =findOne("<id>")= by spreading a string. =count({ traits })= answered 0 because =DataRepository.count= never ran =resolveTraits= — it does now, with =findAndCount · findOneOrFail=.

The siblings: =strike(minted, em, kinds)= instantiates each daemon's repositories by the entity NAME (=resolveTraits= reads =entityName.charAt=), gives each its =entity= namespace (the trait entity halves, =this= the repository) and a non-enumerable =entities= — the daemon's own four — so a trait verb reaches =this.entities.note= and chess's =owner(kind)= reads =this.entities.mode.findOne({ type: "topology", slug: kind })= through the scope. =this.daemon= is the slug; =this.schematics= the daemon's vocabulary; =this.claim(row, name)= the gate over it. =review(row, signal)= loses its third positional: the user is =this.owner()= (the filter params), the notes are =this.entities.note=.

** the twitch — one place, rich, composing

Superseded in the daemon's half by decision row 130 (the daemon BOUND, never passed — =owned()= drops, campaign 2 proved it); the scope half stands. Measured (=probes/subscriber.js=, the design pass's two-argument form): =datamap.registerSubscriber(twitch, daemon)= built =shape.subscriber(twitch, { daemon, scope })= where =scope = (args, task) => RequestContext.create(args.em, task)= — a fresh fork off the EVENT's em keeps the request's filter params (mikro's =fork()= copies =filterParams=, =EntityManager.js:1445=) and drops the transaction context. A foreign daemon's row is dropped before dispatch (=args.meta.properties.daemon= · =args.entity.daemon=); =ctx.daemon= is the FULL live daemon seated by the subscriber's own carry (never a =use= on the twitch); =/before= hearings run on the event (they mutate the row inside the flush); =/after= hearings run DETACHED (=shard.datamap.detached=, added to the twitch's =/after= branch by =registerSubscriber= itself) inside the fresh scope. Three variants measured on two daemons: fresh scope + detached → italian heard =casa= only, spanish =cane= only, the runtime both; the =/after= handler read through =ctx.daemon.entities.literal= and WROTE a note through =ctx.daemon.entities.note= + =flush=, both notes landed, no error. Fresh scope WITHOUT detached → =Knex: Timeout acquiring a connection= (a fork mid-flush deadlocks the one sqlite connection). The event's own context + detached → =You cannot call em.flush() from inside lifecycle hook handlers=, timing-dependent. So: detached AND fresh, built in the one place.

=Twitch= (=prototypes/twitch.js=) is a Vector whose =open= and =graft= COMPOSE at an occupied path (=sequence(first, second)=, each fired by arity): measured, a second =open= and a grafted vector on =/after/literal/create= both fired beside the first. Today's =Vector.open= REPLACES — DATASINK's =/after/literal/update= and =reactive='s =/after/literal/update= (wired on the first =/subscribe=) collide on one daemon twitch, which is the shape of known-issue =daemon /after/literal/update twitch never fires= (=known-issues.org:582=); c2 measures it against the stucatch case before the entry is cut. The kernel twitches (Literal's fold · Buffer's LABELED) stay plain =Vector=s grafted onto the runtime's =Twitch= — their paths are disjoint. =export const twitch= in =Literal.ts= · =Buffer.ts= becomes module-private (two star-exported names made =@vivalence/runtime= export none); the descriptor's =.twitch= is the reach. Unregister on daemon stop: NOT owed — a daemon's life is the runtime's (no composition routes); the ORM closes with the runtime.

** the retention successor — the trait's entity half

=traits.define(name, { schematic, repository, entity })=: =schematic= is the payload's shape; =repository= the verbs over the TABLE; =entity= the readings over a ROW — pure functions =(row, …args)= with =this= the minted repository, exposed as =repository.entity.<reading>= by =strike= and collision-checked by =gather= like verbs (=repository.due= · =entity.assess= are two namespaces). A mode reaches a literal's retention as =ctx.daemon.entities.literal.entity.assess(word)= → the RETAINED note off =word.notes= (populated; an unpopulated collection THROWS naming the query) → =this.entities.note.entity.assess(note, now)= → ={ status, lastSignal, due, strength, is: { virgin, weak, familiar, strong, succeeded, failed } }= or =undefined= when the user holds no RETAINED note. =strength= is each driver's own JS dialect of its =sql.strength= — bayesian =exp(-(hours since lastAt) / halflife)=, boolean =state === true ? 1 : 0=, counter =min(streak / 8, 1)= — =literal.test.js= pins the two dialects equal on the five strength rows. The 94 =.retention= lines move mechanically (the sweep below); nothing on the row, nothing on the schema.

=evolve= writes =due= (ISO of =nextAt=) · =lastAt= (ms) · =lastSignal= beside the driver's result, normalises a string signal, and encodes when =lastAt == null= (a boolean =false= state has no keys — the leg's =Object.keys(held.state).length= re-encoded every review). The claim gate fills the schematic's defaults on a CLONE before judging (=probes/claim.js=: ={}= → ={ driver: "BAYESIAN", status: "UNKNOWN", state: {} }=, the row's payload untouched, a =Date= in a string slot → ={}=), so =review= creates =trait: { RETAINED: {} }= and the gate answers the defaults.

** the recipe — InstanceMask, raw, no scan, the registry

=InstanceMask= (=schematics/primitives/instance.js=) is the authored file: every key named, =additionalProperties: false= at the root (a hand-migrated shelf that still says =lighthouse= is refused with the key named), secrets a ="${SECRET_…}"= reference by pattern, statics free strings, no seat, no default; =populate.recipe= checks it and throws the faults. =Instance= stays the settled unit and gains =runtime.secrets.jwt= (required, =minLength 24=) so a blank jwt is a settle fault, never a dormant provider. The JSON that passes it is =commons/instances/hello-world/instance.json= in the tangle. =instance.raw= is the parsed object taken BEFORE =source= is laid on (=module = { ...own, source: file }=) — no clone, no Path inside, =Daemon.mask= serialises. =expand.scan= dies: nothing read it (the doctor reads =check.environment(instance)= over the read record). The registry walk (=registry.mount=) also walks =instance.json= files and registers them through =read.json= — =viva instance/create hello-world= resolves =@commons/instance/hello-world= again; =commons/fixtures/instance/fixture.viva.js= becomes =instance.json= (c2, with =ghost/tests/create.test.js=). The ledger's slug derives from =ledger.json='s stem (=derive= gained the json arm, PROVEN). =seat()= is NOT extracted — nothing calls it; =resolve.mountpoints= stays as it is minus the lighthouse and the hallucinators fall-through.

** the request context at boot — as the leg wrote it

Daemon =core= · reconcile · the process repositories each run under the scope that owns their em (=die.runtime.datamap.shard.scope= · =die.runtime.processes.datamap.shard.scope=); the runtime aperture carries =shard.datamap.inject(die.runtime.datamap)=; the daemon's die is ={ ...context, controller, mask, daemon }= with =context = { runtime }= from =resolution.daemons=. ** userspace — a turn on a runtime-level thread

Measured (=probes/turn.js=): =scoped("data")= narrowed =thread: "<id>"= into ={ id, user }= and mikro inserted a second Thread (=UNIQUE constraint failed: Thread.id=, the 409). A scope wall on =data= stamps the OWNER column only (=user: ctx.user.id=); a nested wall (=thread: { user }=) is a =where= shape and never a value → =stamp(data, scope)= sets scalar walls, =narrow= stays for =where=. Ownership of the FK is the repository's: =TurnRepository.create({ thread, …fields })= resolves the thread through =em.findOneOrFail(ThreadEntity, thread)= under the request's =user= filter — a foreign thread id is =NotFoundError= (404 through =errors()=), the way =BufferRepository.create= and =TerminalRepository.create= already resolve theirs. =chain= awaits it. What a mode-less thread DOES is fork 8.

** the aperture

The daemon's =/cortex= stays where it is (protected under =/daemon/<slug>=, serving the split cortex); the runtime opens none — nothing consumes a runtime-level LLM route and the open list names no unauthenticated bill (fork 8 (b) would add one under =/userspace=). =aperture.registry= · =aperture.instance= · =/registry= · =/instance= · =/metadata/registry= do not exist and are struck from the law. =/userspace/entities/note= serves the KERNEL note repository (=die.runtime.datamap.entities.note=) over the runtime twitch: a user's notes span daemons, the =user= filter keeps them the user's own or shared; the two domains' =resolve(daemon)= hooks (education's trace · retention branches, chess's trace) die with their entities — no daemon grafts a second note route. =reactive= broadcasts =serialize(entity)= (relations as ids: 342 chars vs 882 with the literal expanded, =probes/turn.js=; the leg measured 104 MB on a populated note) and reads the owner off the EVENT's em (=hook.input.em.getFilterParams=), which is where the write's user lives.

** entities

Buffer is concrete (=class: BufferEntity=, no =abstract=) and =assemble= is =collate= alone — =seal= had no live case. =Activity.mode= is nullable (a runtime-level activity). =Terminal.daemon= exists, so the subscriber's filter passes a terminal to the daemon it is seated on and an unseated one to none — the leg's prose was wrong, the filter right. =Daemon extends BaseEntity= (=ensure= assigns). =Note.literal= · =Literal.notes= as the entity map wrote them; the stray =literals= Collection on Note dies. The scout's four M1 rows without a block are written: =auth.js= (=UserReference= · =AuthResponse.user= · =VerifyResponse.user=), the client descriptors (=user.js= =slug · traits · trait=; =literal.js= gains =daemon=), =Activity.mode= nullable, =prototypes/datamap.js= (one class per kind — nothing to change). =user-byusername-querybuilder=: =this.em.createQueryBuilder(this.entityName, "u")= on 6.6.7 (PROVEN; m78 may hand the repository its own builder back).

** tests

=tests/runtime/boot.test.js= dies (it boots the real repo against the real =~/.viva=; =hello.test.js= boots the rig on a temp ledger and is the gate). The relative import of commons' =config()= in a runtime test stays: commons is the checkout's own package (/"hand knows foot"/), =scenarios/registry.js accio= is for tapped packages, and =accio= would read this machine. =test-rig-recipe=: the boot test writes its recipe — c2 writes it as =instance.json= and boots the shipped =commons/instances/hello-world/instance.json= too. The cross-package gate stays three gates in three homes (chess beside a kernel-only italian · education's italian + spanish · the runtime's inline stand-in domain); a repo test never imports a registry package.

** =subsystems/typology/gestalten/belt/traits.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2, the cast 10-08 · claim · entity · descriptors · collide over two halves · claim fills without a clone of its own
#+begin_src javascript
// subsystems/typology/gestalten/belt/traits.js
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

const INSTALLED = new WeakMap();

export function define(name, { schematic = null, repository = {}, entity = {} } = {}) {
  return { name, schematic, repository, entity };
}

export function claim(row, name, vocabulary) {
  const schema = vocabulary[name];
  if (!schema) throw new Error(`[traits] no trait ${name} in a vocabulary of ${Object.keys(vocabulary).join(" · ") || "nothing"}`);
  const held = row?.traits?.includes(name) ? row.trait?.[name] : undefined;
  if (!held) return {};
  const filled = schema.fill(held);
  return [...schema.errors(filled)][0] ? {} : filled;
}

export function repository(Repository, traits) {
  for (const held of traits)
    for (const [key, descriptor] of Object.entries(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(held.repository)))
      Object.defineProperty(Repository.prototype, key, { ...descriptor, configurable: true });
  return Repository;
}

const daemonOf = (row) => row.daemon?.slug ?? row.daemon ?? null;

export function entity(Entity, traits, daemon = null) {
  const installed = INSTALLED.get(Entity) ?? INSTALLED.set(Entity, { owners: new Map(), vocabularies: new Map() }).get(Entity);
  const vocabulary = installed.vocabularies.get(daemon) ?? installed.vocabularies.set(daemon, {}).get(daemon);
  for (const held of traits) {
    vocabulary[held.name] = held.schematic ?? v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true });
    for (const [key, method] of Object.entries(held.entity)) {
      const owner = installed.owners.get(key);
      if (owner && owner !== held.name) throw new Error(`trait ${held.name}.entity.${key} collides with ${owner}.entity.${key} on ${Entity.name}`);
      installed.owners.set(key, held.name);
      Object.defineProperty(Entity.prototype, key, {
        value(...args) {
          const spoken = installed.vocabularies.get(daemonOf(this)) ?? {};
          if (!spoken[held.name]) {
            throw new Error(`[traits] ${held.name}.${key}: ${Entity.name} ${this.slug ?? this.id} claims no ${held.name} — daemon ${daemonOf(this)} speaks ${Object.keys(spoken).join(" · ") || "nothing"}`);
          }
          return method.call(this, ...args);
        },
        writable: true,
        configurable: true,
      });
    }
  }
  return Entity;
}

export function collide(traits) {
  const seen = new Map();
  for (const held of traits)
    for (const half of ["repository", "entity"])
      for (const key of Object.keys(held[half])) {
        const at = `${half}.${key}`;
        if (seen.has(at)) throw new Error(`trait ${held.name}.${at} collides with ${seen.get(at)}.${at}`);
        seen.set(at, held.name);
      }
  return traits;
}
#+end_src

** =subsystems/typology/prototypes/twitch.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · open/graft compose
#+begin_src javascript
// subsystems/typology/prototypes/twitch.js
import { Vector } from "./vector.js";
import { fire } from "../gestalten/steer/strategy.js";

const sequence = (first, second) => async (ctx) => {
  await fire(first, ctx);
  await fire(second, ctx);
};

export class Twitch extends Vector {
  open(signature, effect) {
    const ear = this.branch(signature);
    ear.effect = ear.effect ? sequence(ear.effect, effect) : effect;
    return this;
  }

  graft(vector) {
    if (vector === this) return this;
    if (vector.effect) this.effect = this.effect ? sequence(this.effect, vector.effect) : vector.effect;
    for (const [nature, edge] of vector.trie) {
      const existing = this.trie.get(nature);
      const ear = existing?.trajectory ?? new this.constructor(this, this.signature);
      ear.graft(edge.trajectory);
      if (existing) existing.pattern = edge.pattern;
      else this.trie.set(nature, { pattern: edge.pattern, trajectory: ear });
    }
    this.carry.push(...vector.carry);
    return this;
  }
}
#+end_src

** =subsystems/paladin/belt/expand.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · no scan
#+begin_src javascript
// subsystems/paladin/belt/expand.js
const REFERENCE = /^\$\{([A-Z0-9_]+)\}$/;
const INLINE = /\$\{([A-Z0-9_]+)\}/g;

export default function expand(paladin) {
  const read = (key) => (key.startsWith("SECRET_") ? paladin.secret.get(key) : paladin.env.get(key));
  const blank = (value) => value === undefined || value === null || value === "";
  const walk = (node, record, at) => {
    if (typeof node === "string") {
      const whole = REFERENCE.exec(node);
      if (whole) {
        const value = read(whole[1]);
        record?.push({ at, read: [whole[1]], unset: blank(value) ? [whole[1]] : [] });
        return value;
      }
      const keys = [...node.matchAll(INLINE)].map((match) => match[1]);
      if (!keys.length) return node;
      const values = Object.fromEntries(keys.map((key) => [key, read(key)]));
      record?.push({ at, read: keys, unset: keys.filter((key) => blank(values[key])) });
      return node.replace(INLINE, (_, key) => values[key] ?? "");
    }
    if (Array.isArray(node)) return node.map((value, index) => walk(value, record, `${at}[${index}]`));
    if (node?.constructor === Object)
      return Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(node).map(([key, value]) => [key, walk(value, record, at ? `${at}.${key}` : key)]),
      );
    return node;
  };
  paladin.expand = (node, record = null, at = "") => walk(node, record, at);
}
#+end_src

** =systems/ghost/trajectories/instance/user.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m0)
#+begin_src javascript
// systems/ghost/trajectories/instance/user.js
import paladin, { lifecycle } from "@vivalence/paladin";
import { Connection } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as jose from "jose";

const ACTIONS = ["signup", "login", "password"];

const operator = (secret) =>
  new jose.SignJWT({ type: "operator" })
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256" })
    .setIssuedAt()
    .setExpirationTime("2m")
    .sign(new TextEncoder().encode(secret));

export async function user(ctx) {
  const [action, username, password] = ctx.signal.params ?? [];
  if (!ACTIONS.includes(action) || !username || !password) {
    return (ctx.effect = { error: "usage: /instance/user <signup|login|password> <username> <password>" });
  }
  await lifecycle.mount(paladin.instance);
  const proof = action === "password" ? { operator: await operator(paladin.secret.get("SECRET_VIVA_JWT")) } : {};
  ctx.effect = await new Connection(paladin.instance.runtime.statics.remote).call(`/lighthouse/${action}`, {
    ...proof,
    username,
    ...(password && { password }),
  });
}
#+end_src

=held= is reset beside =declaration=: the ledger's =Registry= is cached on first access (=subsystems/paladin/prototypes/ledger/ledger.js:25=), so a re-rooted init would otherwise tap into the OLD home's =registry.json=. =tap= is idempotent (=Registry.add=), so a second init writes nothing. Proven: ghost =tests/ledger.test.js= 18/18 (init asserts =effect.commons= and =registry.json = [commons]=; every later record reads =[COMMONS, "pack"]=) · QA =fresh= 9/9 (=registry.json= after =ledger/init= is =[effect.commons]=) · =italian= 8/8 and =reconcile= 5/5 with the hand tap of commons gone · ghost whole suite 97·8 on the dev HOME (the 8 = =create.test.js= machine reads, pre-existing) · 104·1 on the empty HOME (=two-shells=, pre-existing).

#+begin_src yaml
// commons/instances/hello-world/docker-compose.yml
services:
  runtime:
    image: vivalence/runtime:alpine
    env_file: .env
    environment:
      VIVA_INSTANCE_MOUNT: /viva/instance
      VIVA_RUNTIME_ORIGIN: http://0.0.0.0:2501
      PUBLIC_VIVA_RUNTIME_REMOTE: ${PUBLIC_VIVA_RUNTIME_REMOTE:-http://localhost:2501/}
    volumes:
      - ./:/viva/instance
    ports:
      - "2501:2501"
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "wget", "-qO-", "http://localhost:2501/status"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 12

  anima:
    image: vivalence/anima:alpine
    environment:
      PUBLIC_VIVA_RUNTIME_REMOTE: ${PUBLIC_VIVA_RUNTIME_REMOTE:-http://localhost:2501/}
      PUBLIC_VIVA_CLIENT_ANIMA_REMOTE: ${PUBLIC_VIVA_CLIENT_ANIMA_REMOTE:-http://localhost:1794/}
    ports:
      - "1794:1794"
    depends_on:
      runtime:
        condition: service_healthy
#+end_src

        "pattern": "^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.\\-]*://(?:(?:[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=:]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})*@)?(?:\\[(?:[0-9A-Fa-f:.]+|v[0-9A-Fa-f]+\\.[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=:]+)\\]|(?:[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})*)(?::[0-9]*)?(?:/(?:[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=:@]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})*)*(?:\\?(?:(?:[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=:@]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})|[/?])*)?(?:#(?:(?:[A-Za-z0-9\\-._~!$&'()*+,;=:@]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})|[/?])*)?$",
        "title": "RFC 3986 URI with an authority (scheme://…)",
        "description": "Runtime address the browser bundle calls. Reaches it through publish(), not a thunk.",
        "default": "${VIVA_RUNTIME_SERVE}",
        "group": "addresses"
      },
      "SECRET_VIVA_JWT": {
        "type": "string",
        "minLength": 24,
        "description": "Signing secret. Minted by viva instance/init; rotate with: openssl rand -base64 24",
        "group": "keys"
      },
      "SECRET_VIVA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": {
        "type": "string",
        "description": "Anthropic key. Declare either provider, both, or neither — a key left blank leaves its hallucinator dormant.",
        "group": "keys"
      },
      "SECRET_VIVA_OPENROUTER_API_KEY": {
        "type": "string",
        "description": "OpenRouter key. The alternative provider. With neither key the daemon attaches no hallucinator and /hello/agent answers as the bot.",
        "group": "keys"
      }
    },
    "additionalProperties": false
  }
}

#+begin_src typescript
// systems/runtime/entities/Daemon.ts
import { BaseEntity, Collection, EntitySchema, types, type Opt } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { DataRepository } from "@vivalence/typology/entities";
import { ModeEntity, LiteralEntity, SymbolEntity, NoteEntity, UserEntity } from "./index.ts";

export class DaemonRepository extends DataRepository {
  unique(query) {
    return { slug: query.slug };
  }
}

export class DaemonEntity extends BaseEntity {
  slug!: string;
  mask: any & Opt = {};
  traits: string[] & Opt = [];
  trait: any & Opt = {};
  createdAt: Date & Opt = new Date();
  updatedAt: Date & Opt = new Date();

  modes = new Collection<ModeEntity>(this);
  literals = new Collection<LiteralEntity>(this);
  symbols = new Collection<SymbolEntity>(this);
  notes = new Collection<NoteEntity>(this);
  users = new Collection<UserEntity>(this);
}

export const DaemonSchema = new EntitySchema({
  class: DaemonEntity,
  repository: () => DaemonRepository,
  tableName: "Daemon",
  name: "Daemon",
  properties: {
    slug: { type: types.string, primary: true },
    mask: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
    traits: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'[]'` },
    trait: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
    createdAt: { type: types.datetime, onCreate: () => new Date(), defaultRaw: `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` },
    updatedAt: { type: types.datetime, onCreate: () => new Date(), onUpdate: () => new Date(), defaultRaw: `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` },
    modes: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => ModeEntity, mappedBy: (mode) => mode.daemon, hidden: true },
    literals: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => LiteralEntity, mappedBy: (literal) => literal.daemon, hidden: true },
    symbols: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => SymbolEntity, mappedBy: (symbol) => symbol.daemon, hidden: true },
    notes: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => NoteEntity, mappedBy: (note) => note.daemon, hidden: true },
    users: { kind: "m:n", entity: () => UserEntity, mappedBy: (user) => user.daemons, hidden: true },
  },
});

export default { type: "daemon", schema: DaemonSchema, entity: DaemonEntity, repository: DaemonRepository };
#+end_src

** =systems/runtime/entities/Note.ts= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · the daemon filter · literal m:1 · no stray literals · flat 10-08, planned at =systems/runtime/entities/userspace/Note.ts=
#+begin_src typescript
// systems/runtime/entities/Note.ts
import { Collection, EntitySchema, types, type Opt, type Rel } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { DataEntity, DataRepository, DataSchema } from "./index.ts";
import { DaemonEntity, ModeEntity, UserEntity, LiteralEntity, SymbolEntity } from "./index.ts";

export class NoteRepository extends DataRepository {
  unique(query) {
    return { daemon: query.daemon ?? null, mode: query.mode ?? null, user: query.user ?? null, slug: query.slug };
  }
  index(where = {}, { limit = 12 } = {}) {
    return this.find(where, { limit, orderBy: { updatedAt: "DESC" } });
  }
}

export class NoteEntity extends DataEntity {
  slug!: string;
  user?: Rel<UserEntity>;
  daemon?: Rel<DaemonEntity>;
  mode?: Rel<ModeEntity>;
  literal?: Rel<LiteralEntity>;

  symbols = new Collection<SymbolEntity>(this);

  parent?: Rel<NoteEntity>;
  children = new Collection<NoteEntity>(this);

  traits: string[] & Opt = [];
  trait: any & Opt = {};
}

export const NoteSchema = new EntitySchema<NoteEntity, DataEntity>({
  class: NoteEntity,
  extends: DataSchema,
  tableName: "Note",
  name: "Note",
  repository: () => NoteRepository,
  uniques: [
    {
      name: "Note_scope_slug_unique",
      expression: "create unique index `Note_scope_slug_unique` on `Note` (coalesce(`daemon_slug`, ''), coalesce(`mode_id`, ''), coalesce(`user_id`, ''), `slug`)",
    },
  ],
  filters: {
    user: { cond: (args: any) => ({ $or: [{ user: args.user }, { user: null }] }), default: true },
    daemon: { cond: (args: any) => ({ daemon: args.daemon }), default: false },
  },
  properties: {
    slug: { type: types.string },
    daemon: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => DaemonEntity, inversedBy: (daemon) => daemon.notes, nullable: true, updateRule: "cascade", deleteRule: "cascade" },
    mode: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => ModeEntity, inversedBy: (mode) => mode.notes, nullable: true, deleteRule: "cascade" },
    user: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => UserEntity, inversedBy: (user) => user.notes, nullable: true, deleteRule: "cascade" },
    literal: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => LiteralEntity, inversedBy: (literal) => literal.notes, nullable: true, deleteRule: "cascade" },
    parent: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => NoteEntity, nullable: true, deleteRule: "cascade" },
    children: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => NoteEntity, mappedBy: (note) => note.parent },
    traits: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'[]'` },
    trait: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
    symbols: { kind: "m:n", entity: () => SymbolEntity, owner: true, pivotTable: "note_symbols", joinColumns: ["note_entity_id"], inverseJoinColumns: ["symbol_entity_id"] },
  },
});

export default { type: "note", schema: NoteSchema, entity: NoteEntity, repository: NoteRepository };
#+end_src

** =systems/runtime/entities/Terminal.ts= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m1) · moved from typology (PROVEN leg m1, whole file) · flat 10-08, planned at =systems/runtime/entities/identity/Terminal.ts=
#+begin_src typescript
// systems/runtime/entities/Terminal.ts
import { types, EntitySchema, type Opt, type Rel } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { DataEntity, DataSchema, DataRepository } from "@vivalence/typology/entities";
import { UserEntity, DaemonEntity, ThreadEntity } from "./index.ts";

export class TerminalRepository extends DataRepository {
  async create({ user, ...fields }: any) {
    const owner = await this.em.findOneOrFail(UserEntity, user);
    const index = await this.count({ user: owner });
    return super.create({ ...fields, user: owner, index });
  }
}

export class TerminalEntity extends DataEntity {
  user!: Rel<UserEntity>;
  daemon: Rel<DaemonEntity> | null & Opt = null;
  thread: Rel<ThreadEntity> | null & Opt = null;
  index: number & Opt = 0;
  traits: string[] & Opt = [];
  trait: any & Opt = {};
}

export const TerminalSchema = new EntitySchema({
  class: TerminalEntity,
  extends: DataSchema,
  repository: () => TerminalRepository,
  name: "Terminal",
  tableName: "Terminal",
  filters: {
    user: {
      cond: (args: any) => ({ user: args.user }),
      default: true,
    },
  },
  properties: {
    user: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => UserEntity, updateRule: "cascade", deleteRule: "cascade" },
    daemon: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => DaemonEntity, nullable: true, updateRule: "cascade", deleteRule: "set null" },
    thread: { kind: "m:1", entity: () => ThreadEntity, nullable: true, updateRule: "cascade", deleteRule: "set null" },
    index: { type: types.integer, default: 0 },
    traits: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'[]'` },
    trait: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
  },
});

export default {
  type: "terminal",
  schema: TerminalSchema,
  entity: TerminalEntity,
  repository: TerminalRepository,
};
#+end_src

** =systems/runtime/entities/User.ts= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m1 · m2) · flat 10-08, planned at =systems/runtime/entities/identity/User.ts=
#+begin_src typescript
// systems/runtime/entities/User.ts
import { Collection, EntitySchema, types, type Opt } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { DataEntity, DataRepository, DataSchema } from "@vivalence/typology/entities";
import { DaemonEntity, TerminalEntity, ThreadEntity, IntentEntity, NoteEntity } from "./index.ts";

export class AuthenticatorEmbedEntity {
  provider!: string;
  credentials: Record<string, any> & Opt = {};
  tokens: Record<string, string> & Opt = {};
}

export const AuthenticatorEmbedSchema = new EntitySchema({
  class: AuthenticatorEmbedEntity,
  embeddable: true,
  properties: {
    provider: { type: types.string, defaultRaw: `''` },
    credentials: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
    tokens: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
  },
});

export class UserRepository extends DataRepository {
  unique(query) {
    return { slug: query.slug };
  }
  byUsername(username: string) {
    return this.em.createQueryBuilder(this.entityName, "u")
      .where(`json_extract(u.authentication, '$.credentials.username') = ?`, [username])
      .getSingleResult();
  }
}

export class UserEntity extends DataEntity {
  slug!: string;
  authentication: AuthenticatorEmbedEntity & Opt = {};
  traits: string[] & Opt = [];
  trait: any & Opt = {};

  daemons = new Collection<DaemonEntity>(this);
  terminals = new Collection<TerminalEntity>(this);
  threads = new Collection<ThreadEntity>(this);
  intents = new Collection<IntentEntity>(this);
  notes = new Collection<NoteEntity>(this);
}

export const UserSchema = new EntitySchema<UserEntity, DataEntity>({
  class: UserEntity,
  extends: DataSchema,
  repository: () => UserRepository,
  tableName: "User",
  name: "User",
  properties: {
    slug: { type: types.string, unique: true },
    authentication: { kind: "embedded", entity: "AuthenticatorEmbedEntity", object: true, defaultRaw: `'{}'`, hidden: true },
    traits: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'[]'` },
    trait: { type: types.json, defaultRaw: `'{}'` },
    daemons: { kind: "m:n", entity: () => DaemonEntity, owner: true, pivotTable: "User_daemons" },
    terminals: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => TerminalEntity, mappedBy: (terminal) => terminal.user },
    threads: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => ThreadEntity, mappedBy: (thread) => thread.user },
    intents: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => IntentEntity, mappedBy: (intent) => intent.user },
    notes: { kind: "1:m", entity: () => NoteEntity, mappedBy: (note) => note.user },
  },
});

export const authenticator = { type: "authenticator", schema: AuthenticatorEmbedSchema, entity: AuthenticatorEmbedEntity };
export default { type: "user", schema: UserSchema, entity: UserEntity, repository: UserRepository };
#+end_src

// systems/runtime/entities/traits.js
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { traits as trait } from "@vivalence/typology";

const LABELED = trait.define("LABELED", { schematic: v.primitives.Label });

export const traits = {
  literal: [LABELED],
  symbol: [
    LABELED,
    trait.define("ONTOLOGICAL", { schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }) }),
    trait.define("STRUCTURAL", { schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }) }),
    trait.define("TOPOGRAPHICAL", { schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }) }),
  ],
  note: [LABELED],
  mode: [],
};

** =systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/mint.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · the filter on every verb · claim · strike
#+begin_src javascript
// systems/runtime/gestalten/belt/mint.js
import { traits as trait } from "@vivalence/typology";

const named = (name, Class) => Object.defineProperty(Class, "name", { value: name });
const capitalize = (word) => (word ?? "runtime").charAt(0).toUpperCase() + (word ?? "runtime").slice(1).replace(/-(\w)/g, (_, c) => c.toUpperCase());

export const gather = (kernel, domain, modes, kind) =>
  trait.collide(
    [...kernel[kind], ...(domain?.traits?.[kind] ?? []), ...modes.flatMap((mode) => mode.traits?.[kind] ?? [])].filter(
      (held, index, all) => all.findIndex((other) => other.name === held.name) === index,
    ),
  );

export const mint = ({ slug, kinds, traits }) =>
  Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(kinds).map(([kind, descriptor]) => {
      const held = traits[kind] ?? [];
      const schematics = Object.fromEntries(held.filter((each) => each.schematic).map((each) => [each.name, each.schematic]));
      const Repository = named(
        `${capitalize(slug)}${capitalize(kind)}Repository`,
        class extends descriptor.repository {
          scope(options = {}) {
            return { ...options, filters: { ...(options.filters ?? {}), daemon: { daemon: slug } } };
          }
          find(where, options) { return super.find(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findOne(where, options) { return super.findOne(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findOneOrFail(where, options) { return super.findOneOrFail(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findAll(options) { return super.findAll(this.scope(options)); }
          findAndCount(where, options) { return super.findAndCount(where, this.scope(options)); }
          findByCursor(where, options) { return super.findByCursor(where, this.scope(options)); }
          count(where, options) { return super.count(where, this.scope(options)); }
          nativeUpdate(where, data, options) { return super.nativeUpdate(where, data, this.scope(options)); }
          nativeDelete(where, options) { return super.nativeDelete(where, this.scope(options)); }
          create(data, options) { return super.create({ ...data, daemon: slug }, options); }
          upsert(data, options) { return super.upsert({ ...data, daemon: slug }, options); }
          unique(query) { return { ...super.unique(query), daemon: slug }; }
          claim(row, name) { return trait.claim(row, name, schematics); }
        },
      );
      trait.repository(Repository, held);
      Object.defineProperties(Repository.prototype, { daemon: { value: slug }, schematics: { value: schematics } });
      return [kind, { repository: Repository, traits: held, schematics }];
    }),
  );

export const strike = (minted, em, kinds) => {
  const struck = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(minted).map(([kind, held]) => [kind, new held.repository(em, kinds[kind].entity.name)]));
  for (const [kind, held] of Object.entries(minted)) {
    trait.entity(kinds[kind].entity, held.traits, struck[kind].daemon);
    Object.defineProperty(struck[kind], "entities", { value: struck });
  }
  return struck;
};
#+end_src

** =systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/aperture/cortex.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2
#+begin_src javascript
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/aperture/cortex.js
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

const { Request, Choice, Tier, Tune } = v.primitives.hallucination;

const ROUND = v.object({
  type: v.string(),
  tune: v.union([Tier, Tune]).optional(),
  request: v.union([Request, Choice.Round]),
});

function validate(input) {
  const round = v.cast(ROUND, { ...input });
  const failure = [...v.errors(ROUND, round)][0];
  if (failure) throw new Error(`[cortex] invalid round ${failure.path}: ${failure.message}`);
  return round;
}

export const cortex = async (die, next) => {
  const round = (via) => async (ctx) => {
    const { type, tune, request } = validate(ctx.input);
    const faculty = die.runtime.cortex.findOne({ type, tune, via });
    if (!faculty?.via?.[via]) throw new Error(`[cortex] no '${type}' faculty resolves a '${via}' avenue`);
    ctx.output = await faculty.via[via](request);
  };

  die.runtime.aperture
    .branch("/cortex")
    .open("/render", round("render"))
    .open("/stream", round("stream"));
  await next();
};
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/aperture/userspace.js
import { shard, v } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const userspace = async (die, next) => {
  const { entities, twitch, lighthouse } = die.runtime;
  const kernel = die.runtime.datamap.entities;
  const owned = die.runtime.aperture.branch("/userspace").use(lighthouse.authenticate).use(lighthouse.bind).branch("/entities");

  owned
    .branch("/terminal")
    .use(shard.datamap.scope((ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id })))
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.terminal))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.terminal, twitch, { scope: (ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id }), filter: "user" }));

  owned
    .branch("/intent")
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.intent))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.intent, twitch));

  owned
    .branch("/thread")
    .use(shard.datamap.scope((ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id })))
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.thread))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.thread, twitch));

  owned
    .branch("/buffer")
    .use(shard.datamap.scope((ctx) => ({ thread: { user: ctx.user.id } })))
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.buffer))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.buffer, twitch, { scope: (ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id }) }));

  owned
    .branch("/turn")
    .use(shard.datamap.scope((ctx) => ({ thread: { user: ctx.user.id } })))
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.turn))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.turn, twitch, { scope: (ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id }) }));

  owned
    .branch("/note")
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(kernel.note))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(kernel.note, twitch, { scope: (ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id }) }));

  const activity = owned
    .branch("/activity")
    .use(shard.datamap.scope((ctx) => ({ user: ctx.user.id })))
    .graft(shard.datamap.repository(entities.activity, { only: ["find", "findOne", "findOneOrFail", "findAndCount", "count"] }))
    .graft(shard.datamap.reactive(entities.activity, twitch));

  const one = activity.branch("/:id").use(async (ctx, next) => {
    ctx.activity = await entities.activity.findOneOrFail({ id: ctx.params.id, user: ctx.user.id });
    await next();
  });
  one.open({ nature: "stdout", yields: v.primitives.controller.Record }, (ctx) => {
    ctx.output = ctx.activity.stdout(ctx.request.raw?.signal);
  });
  const stdin = one.branch("/stdin");
  for (const name of Object.keys(v.primitives.controller.MACHINE.signals))
    stdin.open(name, async (ctx) => {
      await ctx.activity.stdin[name](ctx.input);
      return ctx.activity.controller.toJSON();
    });

  await next();
};
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/lighthouse/auth.js
import { Vector, shard } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { wrap } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import * as argon2 from "argon2";
import { bearer, identify, signer } from "./signer.js";

const respond = {
  success: (data = {}) => ({ status: "SUCCESS", ...data }),
  error: (ctx, code, message, httpStatus = 400) => {
    ctx.response.status = httpStatus;
    return { status: "ERROR", error: { code, message } };
  },
};

const card = (user) => ({ id: user.id, slug: user.slug, traits: user.traits, trait: user.trait });

const authority = async (ctx, user) => {
  const sign = signer(ctx.lighthouse.secret);
  return { access: await sign.access(user), refresh: await sign.refresh(user) };
};

export const auth = (lighthouse) => new Vector().use(shard.context.bind("lighthouse", lighthouse))
  .open("/signup", async ({ username, password }, ctx) => {
    if (!username || !password) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_INPUT", "Username and password required");
    const existing = await ctx.lighthouse.users.byUsername(username);
    if (existing) return respond.error(ctx, "USERNAME_EXISTS", "Username already taken");

    const user = ctx.lighthouse.users.create({ slug: username });
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    const tokens = await authority(ctx, user);
    wrap(user).assign({
      authentication: { provider: "password", credentials: { username, password: await argon2.hash(password) }, tokens },
    });
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    return respond.success({ user: card(user), authority: tokens });
  })
  .open("/login", async ({ username, password }, ctx) => {
    if (!username || !password) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_INPUT", "Username and password required");
    const user = await identify(ctx.lighthouse, { username, password });
    if (!user) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_CREDENTIALS", "Invalid username or password", 401);
    const tokens = await authority(ctx, user);
    wrap(user).assign({ authentication: { tokens } }, { merge: true, mergeObjectProperties: true });
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    return respond.success({ authority: tokens, user: card(user) });
  })
  .open("/password", async ({ operator, username, password }, ctx) => {
    if (!operator || !username || !password) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_INPUT", "Operator token, username and password required");
    if (!(await signer(ctx.lighthouse.secret).verify(operator, "operator"))) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_TOKEN", "Invalid operator token", 401);
    const user = await ctx.lighthouse.users.byUsername(username);
    if (!user) return respond.error(ctx, "USER_NOT_FOUND", "User not found", 404);
    wrap(user).assign(
      { authentication: { credentials: { password: await argon2.hash(password) }, tokens: { access: null, refresh: null } } },
      { merge: true, mergeObjectProperties: true },
    );
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    return respond.success({ user: card(user) });
  })
  .open("/logout", async ({ refresh }, ctx) => {
    if (!refresh) return respond.error(ctx, "MISSING_TOKEN", "Refresh token required");
    const user = await bearer(ctx.lighthouse, refresh);
    if (!user) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_TOKEN", "Invalid refresh token", 401);
    wrap(user).assign({ authentication: { tokens: { access: null, refresh: null } } }, { mergeObjectProperties: true });
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    return respond.success({ message: "Logged out" });
  })
  .open("/verify", async ({ access }, ctx) => {
    if (!access) return respond.error(ctx, "MISSING_TOKEN", "Access token required");
    const payload = await signer(ctx.lighthouse.secret).verify(access, "access");
    if (!payload) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_TOKEN", "Invalid or expired token", 401);
    const user = await ctx.lighthouse.users.findOne({ id: payload.id });
    if (!user) return respond.error(ctx, "USER_NOT_FOUND", "User not found", 401);
    return respond.success({ user: card(user) });
  })
  .open("/refresh", async ({ refresh }, ctx) => {
    if (!refresh) return respond.error(ctx, "MISSING_TOKEN", "Refresh token required");
    const user = await bearer(ctx.lighthouse, refresh);
    if (!user) return respond.error(ctx, "INVALID_TOKEN", "Invalid or revoked refresh token", 401);
    const access = await signer(ctx.lighthouse.secret).access(user);
    wrap(user).assign({ authentication: { tokens: { access } } }, { merge: true, mergeObjectProperties: true });
    await ctx.lighthouse.users.em.flush();
    return respond.success({ access });
  });
#+end_src

** =systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/lighthouse/signer.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m2)
#+begin_src javascript
// systems/runtime/lifecycle/runtime/lighthouse/signer.js
import * as argon2 from "argon2";
import * as jose from "jose";

const ACCESS_TTL = "1d";
const REFRESH_TTL = "180d";
const OPERATOR_TTL = "2m";

const refuse = (ctx, code, message) => {
  ctx.response.status = 401;
  ctx.response.body = { status: "ERROR", error: { code, message } };
};

export function signer(secret) {
  const key = new TextEncoder().encode(secret);
  const sign = (payload, expiration) =>
    new jose.SignJWT(payload).setProtectedHeader({ alg: "HS256" }).setIssuedAt().setExpirationTime(expiration).sign(key);
  return {
    access: (user) => sign({ id: user.id, type: "access" }, ACCESS_TTL),
    refresh: (user) => sign({ id: user.id, type: "refresh" }, REFRESH_TTL),
    operator: () => sign({ type: "operator" }, OPERATOR_TTL),
    verify: async (token, type) => {
      const payload = await jose.jwtVerify(token, key).then((result) => result.payload).catch(() => null);
      return payload?.type === type ? payload : null;
    },
  };
}

export async function identify({ secret, users }, { username, password }) {
  if (!username || !password) return null;
  const user = await users.byUsername(username);
  if (!user?.authentication?.credentials?.password) return null;
  const valid = await argon2.verify(user.authentication.credentials.password, password);
  return valid ? user : null;
}

export async function bearer({ secret, users }, refresh) {
  const payload = await signer(secret).verify(refresh, "refresh");
  if (!payload) return null;
  const user = await users.findOne({ id: payload.id });
  return user?.authentication?.tokens?.refresh === refresh ? user : null;
}

export function authenticate({ secret, users }) {
  return async (ctx, next) => {
    if (ctx.user) return await next();
    const header = ctx.request.headers?.get("authorization");
    const token = header?.startsWith("Bearer ") ? header.split(" ")[1] : ctx.request.url.searchParams?.get("token");
    if (!token) return refuse(ctx, "MISSING_TOKEN", "Authorization header or token param required");
    const payload = await signer(secret).verify(token, "access");
    if (!payload) return refuse(ctx, "INVALID_TOKEN", "Invalid or expired token");
    ctx.user = await users.findOne({ id: payload.id });
    if (!ctx.user) return refuse(ctx, "USER_NOT_FOUND", "User not found");
    await next();
  };
}
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/literal/claims.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

const open = () => v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true });

export const TRANSLATED = traits.define("TRANSLATED", {
  schematic: v.object({ known: v.string(), learning: v.string() }, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    get card() {
      const repository = this;
      return {
        fields: ["slug", "known", "learning", "ontology", "status"],
        populate: ["notes"],
        project: (literal) => ({
          slug: literal.slug,
          known: literal.trait?.TRANSLATED?.known ?? "",
          learning: literal.trait?.TRANSLATED?.learning ?? "",
          ontology: literal.ontology,
          status: literal.assess()?.status ?? "UNTOUCHED",
        }),
      };
    },
    search(query) {
      const like = `%${query.search}%`;
      return [
        { slug: { $like: like } },
        { trait: { TRANSLATED: { known: { $like: like } } } },
        { trait: { TRANSLATED: { learning: { $like: like } } } },
      ];
    },
  },
});

export const EXEMPLIFIED = traits.define("EXEMPLIFIED", { schematic: open() });

export const VOCALIZED = traits.define("VOCALIZED", { schematic: open() });

export const CONJUGATED = traits.define("CONJUGATED", {
  schematic: open(),
  repository: {
    async family(rows) {
      const words = rows.filter((row) => row.ontology === "word");
      if (!words.length) return [];
      const seen = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.slug));
      const paradigms = (await this.find({ uses: { $in: words.map((row) => row.id) }, ontology: "conjugation" }, { limit: 10, populate: ["notes"] })).filter(
        (paradigm) => !seen.has(paradigm.slug),
      );
      if (!paradigms.length) return [];
      const wanted = [...new Set(paradigms.map((paradigm) => paradigm.trait?.CONJUGATED?.infinitive).filter(Boolean))].filter((slug) => !seen.has(slug));
      const infinitives = wanted.length ? await this.find({ slug: { $in: wanted } }, { populate: ["notes"] }) : [];
      return paradigms.map((paradigm) => ({
        paradigm,
        infinitive: infinitives.find((row) => row.slug === paradigm.trait?.CONJUGATED?.infinitive) ?? null,
      }));
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

** =~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/literal/ranked.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m3)
#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/literal/ranked.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const UNRANKED = 999999;

export const rankOf = (trait) => {
  const ranked = trait?.RANKED;
  if (!ranked) return UNRANKED;
  if (Number.isInteger(ranked.rank) && ranked.rank >= 1) return ranked.rank;
  if (typeof ranked.zipf === "number" && ranked.zipf > 0) return Math.round(10 ** (9 - ranked.zipf));
  return UNRANKED;
};

export const RANKED = traits.define("RANKED", { schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }) });
#+end_src
