#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/literal/retained.js
import { raw } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { object, is } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { formula } from "../note/retained.js";
import { UNRANKED } from "./ranked.js";

const RANK = raw(`coalesce(json_extract(l0.trait, '$.RANKED.rank'), ${UNRANKED})`);
const STRENGTH = (owner) =>
  raw(
    `(select ${formula("n")} from Note n where n.literal_id = l0.id${owner ? ` and n.user_id = '${owner}'` : ""} and exists (select 1 from json_each(n.traits) where value = 'RETAINED'))`,
  );
const held = () => ({ $ne: null });

const retained = (literal) => {
  if (!literal.notes?.isInitialized?.())
    throw new Error(`[education] ${literal.slug}: RETAINED is read off literal.notes — populate: ["notes"] on the query that fetched it`);
  return literal.notes.getItems().find((note) => note.traits.includes("RETAINED"));
};

export const RETAINED = traits.define("RETAINED", {
  repository: {
    owner() {
      const params = this.getEntityManager().getContext().getFilterParams("user");
      return params?.user ? { user: params.user } : {};
    },

    constrain(where) {
      if (!where || !is.array(where.symbols)) return where;
      const { symbols, ...rest } = where;
      if (!symbols.length) return rest;
      const constraints = symbols.map((slug) => ({ symbols: { slug } }));
      return { ...rest, $and: [...(rest.$and || []), ...constraints] };
    },

    async feed(where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      const due = await this.due(where, { limit, blacklist, populate });
      if (due.length >= limit) return due.slice(0, limit);

      const novel = await this.novel(where, {
        limit: limit - due.length,
        blacklist: { literals: [...(blacklist?.literals || []), ...due.map((d) => d.id)] },
        populate,
      });
      return [...due, ...novel];
    },

    async novel(where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      const filter = object.merge(
        { notes: { $none: { ...this.owner(), trait: { RETAINED: held() } } } },
        { id: { $nin: blacklist?.literals || [] } },
        this.constrain(where),
      );
      return this.find(filter, { orderBy: { [RANK]: "ASC" }, limit, populate: populate ? [...populate, "notes"] : ["notes"] });
    },

    async due(where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      const filter = object.merge(
        { notes: { trait: { RETAINED: { due: { $lt: new Date().toISOString() } } }, ...this.owner() } },
        { id: { $nin: blacklist?.literals || [] } },
        this.constrain(where),
      );
      return this.find(filter, { populate: populate ? [...populate, "notes"] : ["notes"], limit });
    },

    async byStrength(where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      return this.find(
        object.merge({ notes: { ...this.owner(), trait: { RETAINED: held() } } }, { id: { $nin: blacklist?.literals || [] } }, this.constrain(where)),
        { populate: populate ? [...populate, "notes"] : ["notes"], orderBy: { [STRENGTH(this.owner().user)]: "ASC" }, limit },
      );
    },

    async byLastSignal(signals, where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      return this.find(
        object.merge(
          { notes: { trait: { RETAINED: { lastSignal: { $in: signals } } }, ...this.owner() } },
          { id: { $nin: blacklist?.literals || [] } },
          this.constrain(where),
        ),
        { populate: populate ? [...populate, "notes"] : ["notes"], limit },
      );
    },

    async sample(where = {}, opts = {}) {
      const { status, limit, blacklist, populate } = opts || {};
      return this.find(
        object.merge(
          status
            ? { notes: { trait: { RETAINED: { status: { $in: status } } }, ...this.owner() } }
            : { notes: { ...this.owner(), trait: { RETAINED: held() } } },
          { id: { $nin: blacklist?.literals || [] } },
          this.constrain(where),
        ),
        { populate: populate ? [...populate, "notes"] : ["notes"], orderBy: { [raw("random()")]: "asc" }, limit },
      );
    },

    async review(row, signal) {
      const notes = this.entities.note;
      const note = (await notes.retention(row)) ??
        notes.create({ ...this.owner(), literal: row, slug: `retention.${row.slug}`, traits: ["RETAINED"], trait: { RETAINED: {} } });
      notes.evolve(note, signal);
      const { status, due } = note.trait.RETAINED;
      notes.create({ ...this.owner(), literal: row, parent: note, slug: `trace.${crypto.randomUUID()}`, traits: ["TRACED"], trait: { TRACED: { signal, snapshot: { status, due } } } });
      await this.em.flush();
      return note;
    },
  },
  entity: {
    assess(now = Date.now()) {
      const note = retained(this);
      return note ? note.assess(now) : undefined;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

** =~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/note/retained.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · entity.assess · evolve fresh = lastAt == null · claim
#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/education/domain/traits/note/retained.js
import { raw } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as retention from "../../retention/index.js";
import { STATUS } from "../../schematics.js";

const at = (field, alias = "n0") => `json_extract(${alias}.trait, '$.RETAINED.${field}')`;
export const formula = (alias) =>
  `CASE ${at("driver", alias)} ${Object.entries(retention.drivers).map(([name, driver]) => `WHEN '${name}' THEN ${driver.sql.strength((field) => at(field, alias))}`).join(" ")} END`;
export const strength = (alias) => raw(formula(alias));
const DUE = raw(at("due"));
const STRENGTH = strength("n0");

const virgin = (held) => !held.status || held.status === "UNTOUCHED";

const schematic = v.object({
  driver: v.enum(Object.keys(retention.drivers)).default("BAYESIAN"),
  status: v.enum(STATUS).default("UNKNOWN"),
  type: v.enum(["INDIVIDUAL", "COLLECTIVE"]).default("INDIVIDUAL"),
  lastSignal: v.string().optional(),
  lastAt: v.number().optional(),
  due: v.string().optional(),
  state: v.any().default({}),
});

export const RETAINED = traits.define("RETAINED", {
  schematic,
  repository: {
    retention(row) {
      return this.findOne({ literal: row, traits: "RETAINED" });
    },
    evolve(note, signal) {
      const held = this.claim(note, "RETAINED");
      const driver = retention.drivers[held.driver];
      const given = typeof signal === "string" ? { enum: signal } : signal;
      const result = held.lastAt == null ? driver.encode(given) : driver.evolve(given, held);
      note.trait = {
        ...note.trait,
        RETAINED: { ...held, ...result, due: new Date(result.nextAt).toISOString(), lastAt: Date.now(), lastSignal: given.enum },
      };
      return note;
    },
    due(where = {}, opts) {
      return this.find({ ...where, traits: "RETAINED", [DUE]: { $lte: new Date().toISOString() } }, { ...opts, orderBy: { [DUE]: "asc" } });
    },
    byStrength(where = {}, opts) {
      return this.find({ ...where, traits: "RETAINED" }, { ...opts, orderBy: { [STRENGTH]: "asc" } });
    },
  },
  entity: {
    assess(now = Date.now()) {
      const held = traits.claim(this, "RETAINED", { RETAINED: schematic });
      const strength = held.lastAt == null ? 0 : retention.drivers[held.driver].strength(held, now);
      return {
        status: held.status,
        lastSignal: held.lastSignal ?? null,
        due: held.due ?? null,
        strength,
        is: {
          virgin: virgin(held),
          weak: held.status === "UNKNOWN" || held.status === "LEARNING",
          familiar: held.status === "LEARNING" || held.status === "KNOWN",
          strong: held.status === "KNOWN" || held.status === "GRADUATED",
          succeeded: held.lastSignal === "SUCCESS" || held.lastSignal === "MASTERY",
          failed: held.lastSignal === "FAILURE" || held.lastSignal === "MISTAKE",
        },
      };
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

** =~/.viva/registry/education/domain/twitch.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 · Twitch · ctx.daemon.entities in the after hearing
#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/education/domain/twitch.js
import { Twitch } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { rankOf } from "./traits/literal/ranked.js";

const annotated = (entity) => (entity.trait?.ANNOTATED?.tokens ?? []).map((token) => token.literal).filter(Boolean);

const conjugated = (entity) => {
  const held = entity.trait?.CONJUGATED;
  if (!held) return [];
  return [...Object.values(held.paradigm ?? {}), held.infinitive].filter(Boolean);
};

export const twitch = new Twitch();

for (const op of ["create", "update"]) {
  twitch.open(`/before/literal/${op}`, (ctx) => {
    const { entity } = ctx.input;
    if (entity.trait?.RANKED) entity.trait = { ...entity.trait, RANKED: { ...entity.trait.RANKED, rank: rankOf(entity.trait) } };
  });

  twitch.open(`/after/literal/${op}`, async (ctx) => {
    const slugs = [...new Set([...annotated(ctx.input.entity), ...conjugated(ctx.input.entity)])];
    if (!slugs.length) return;
    const { literal } = ctx.daemon.entities;
    const entity = await literal.findOneOrFail({ id: ctx.input.entity.id }, { populate: ["uses"] });
    const used = await literal.find({ slug: { $in: slugs } });
    const missing = used.filter((row) => !entity.uses.contains(row));
    if (!missing.length) return;
    entity.uses.add(missing);
    await literal.em.flush();
  });
}
#+end_src

// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/analysed.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const ANALYSED = traits.define("ANALYSED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    async analysed(game, report) {
      game.trait = { ...game.trait, ANALYSED: report };
      if (!game.traits.includes("ANALYSED")) game.traits = [...game.traits, "ANALYSED"];
      await this.em.flush();
      return game;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/catalogued.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as rules from "../../rules/index.js";

export const CATALOGUED = traits.define("CATALOGUED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    async classify(fens) {
      const epds = fens.map((fen) => rules.epd(fen));
      const rows = await this.find({ ontology: "opening", trait: { CATALOGUED: { epd: { $in: epds } } } });
      const byEpd = new Map(rows.map((row) => [row.trait.CATALOGUED.epd, row]));
      for (let index = epds.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
        const hit = byEpd.get(epds[index]);
        if (hit) {
          const { eco, name, pgn } = hit.trait.CATALOGUED;
          return { eco, name, pgn, lastBookPly: index, opening: hit };
        }
      }
      return null;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/evaluated.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

const EVALUATIONS_KEPT = 8;

export const EVALUATED = traits.define("EVALUATED", {
  schematic: v.array(v.any()),
  repository: {
    evaluated(position, { depth = 0 } = {}) {
      const held = position.trait?.EVALUATED ?? [];
      return held.find((entry) => (entry.depth ?? 0) >= depth) ?? null;
    },

    async record(position, evaluation) {
      const held = position.trait?.EVALUATED ?? [];
      position.trait = { ...position.trait, EVALUATED: [evaluation, ...held].slice(0, EVALUATIONS_KEPT) };
      if (!position.traits.includes("EVALUATED")) position.traits = [...position.traits, "EVALUATED"];
      await this.em.flush();
      return evaluation;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/laddered.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";

export const LADDERED = traits.define("LADDERED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    rung(position, elo) {
      return position.trait?.LADDERED?.[String(elo)] ?? null;
    },

    async ladder(position, { elo, plays, engine, movetime }) {
      const held = this.rung(position, elo);
      const entry = { plays: [...(held?.plays ?? []), ...plays], engine: engine ?? held?.engine ?? null, movetime: movetime ?? held?.movetime ?? null, at: new Date().toISOString() };
      position.trait = { ...position.trait, LADDERED: { ...(position.trait?.LADDERED ?? {}), [String(elo)]: entry } };
      if (!position.traits.includes("LADDERED")) position.traits = [...position.traits, "LADDERED"];
      await this.em.flush();
      return entry;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/placed.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as rules from "../../rules/index.js";

export const PLACED = traits.define("PLACED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    card: {
      fields: ["slug", "ontology", "traits"],
      populate: [],
      project: (literal) => ({
        slug: literal.slug,
        ontology: literal.ontology,
        ...(literal.trait?.PLACED && { fen: literal.trait.PLACED.fen }),
        ...(literal.trait?.RECORDED && { plies: literal.trait.RECORDED.plies, result: literal.trait.TERMINATED?.result }),
        ...(literal.trait?.CATALOGUED && { eco: literal.trait.CATALOGUED.eco, name: literal.trait.CATALOGUED.name }),
        ...(literal.trait?.POSED && { rating: literal.trait.POSED.rating, level: literal.trait.POSED.level }),
      }),
    },

    search(query) {
      const like = `%${query.search}%`;
      return [
        { slug: { $like: like } },
        { trait: { CATALOGUED: { name: { $like: like } } } },
        { trait: { RECORDED: { tags: { White: { $like: like } } } } },
        { trait: { RECORDED: { tags: { Black: { $like: like } } } } },
        { trait: { RECORDED: { tags: { Event: { $like: like } } } } },
      ];
    },

    async owner(kind) {
      const row = await this.entities.mode.findOne({ type: "topology", slug: kind });
      if (!row) throw new Error(`[chess] the kernel mounts no @chess/topology/${kind} — nothing may own a ${kind} row`);
      return row;
    },

    async symbolsNamed(slugs) {
      const wanted = [...new Set(slugs.filter(Boolean))];
      if (!wanted.length) return [];
      return this.entities.symbol.find({ slug: { $in: wanted } });
    },

    async mint(kind, slug, data, symbols) {
      const owner = await this.owner(kind);
      const held = await this.findOne({ slug, mode: owner.id });
      if (held) return held;
      const row = this.create({ slug, mode: owner, ...data });
      for (const symbol of await this.symbolsNamed([kind, ...symbols])) row.symbols.add(symbol);
      await this.em.flush();
      return row;
    },

    async reach(fen) {
      const canonical = rules.fen(rules.position(fen));
      const slug = await rules.key(canonical);
      const { material, pieces } = rules.material(canonical);
      const phase = rules.phase(canonical);
      return this.mint(
        "position",
        slug,
        {
          traits: ["PLACED"],
          trait: { PLACED: { fen: canonical, epd: rules.epd(canonical), key: slug, material, pieces, phase } },
        },
        [`position.phase.${phase}`, pieces <= 7 ? `position.endgame.pieces.${pieces}` : null],
      );
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/posed.js
import { raw } from "@mikro-orm/core";
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as rules from "../../rules/index.js";
import { level as levelOf } from "../../schematics.js";

export const POSED = traits.define("POSED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    async puzzle({ level, themes, exclude = [] } = {}) {
      const where = { ontology: "puzzle" };
      const symbols = [];
      if (level) symbols.push(`puzzle.level.${level}`);
      for (const theme of themes ?? []) symbols.push(`puzzle.theme.${theme}`);
      if (symbols.length) where.symbols = symbols;
      if (exclude.length) where.id = { $nin: exclude };
      return this.findOne(where, { orderBy: { [raw("random()")]: "asc" } });
    },

    async pose({ id, fen, solution, rating, themes = [], plays = 0, source }) {
      const canonical = rules.fen(rules.position(fen));
      rules.replay({ fen: canonical, moves: solution });
      const slug = `puzzle-${id}`;
      const band = levelOf(rating);
      return this.mint(
        "puzzle",
        slug,
        {
          traits: ["POSED", ...(source ? ["SOURCED"] : [])],
          trait: {
            POSED: { fen: canonical, solution, rating, themes, plays, level: band },
            ...(source ? { SOURCED: { ...source, importedAt: new Date().toISOString() } } : {}),
          },
        },
        [`puzzle.level.${band}`, ...themes.map((theme) => `puzzle.theme.${theme}`)],
      );
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

** =~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/recorded.js= — new · PROVEN — campaign 2 (leg m3)
#+begin_src javascript
// ~/.viva/registry/chess/domain/traits/literal/recorded.js
import { traits } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { v } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as rules from "../../rules/index.js";

export const RECORDED = traits.define("RECORDED", {
  schematic: v.object({}, { additionalProperties: true }),
  repository: {
    async seal({
      tags = {},
      moves,
      initial = rules.START,
      variant = "standard",
      result,
      reason,
      seats,
      clock,
      source,
      opening,
      variations = [],
    }) {
      const line = rules.replay({ fen: initial, moves, variant });
      const slug = await rules.digest(`${variant}|${initial}|${moves.join(" ")}`);
      const known = result ?? line.outcome?.result ?? tags.Result ?? "*";
      const why = reason ?? (line.checkmate ? "checkmate" : line.stalemate ? "stalemate" : line.insufficient ? "insufficient" : null);
      const { text: pgn } = rules.pgn.make({ tags: { ...tags, Result: known }, moves, initial, variant, variations });
      const traits = ["RECORDED", "TERMINATED"];
      const trait = {
        RECORDED: { tags: { ...tags, Result: known }, pgn, moves, initial, variant, plies: moves.length, ...(variations.length && { variations }) },
        TERMINATED: { result: known, reason: why },
      };
      if (seats) (traits.push("SEATED"), (trait.SEATED = seats));
      if (clock) (traits.push("CLOCKED"), (trait.CLOCKED = clock));
      if (source) (traits.push("SOURCED"), (trait.SOURCED = { ...source, importedAt: new Date().toISOString() }));
      if (opening) (traits.push("OPENED"), (trait.OPENED = opening));
      const resultSymbol = known === "1-0" ? "white" : known === "0-1" ? "black" : known === "1/2-1/2" ? "draw" : "unfinished";
      const row = await this.mint("game", slug, { traits, trait }, [
        `game.variant.${variant}`,
        `game.result.${resultSymbol}`,
        why ? `game.termination.${why}` : null,
        opening?.eco ? `opening.eco.${opening.eco}` : null,
      ]);
      return row;
    },
  },
});
#+end_src

* tests — land from the captured rig

The test side of the flag day, M3, M5 and M6 is never inlined: every row lands from the captured rig, =~/.viva/bak/ikiro/m80-campaign-20261007/rig/<path>= — a =~/.viva/registry/<pkg>/…= row from =…/rig/campaign/registry/<pkg>/…=. =new= copies the file in · =edit= replaces it whole (the base is the repo's, or the published package's, as of 10-08) · =delete= removes it. =count= is the file's tests and steps, passed/total, on the census of 10-08 (=HOME= empty; the registry suites from the rig root with =--config deno.jsonc=); =—= a helper, a fixture or a deleted file. Five runtime files reach =@education= through =accio= and read the PUBLISHED package (=registry-accio-tests-machine=): red on the census, green on a ledger that taps the rig's copies — they turn green when the registry lands with the flag day. Three snapshot demos (paladin =paladin-instance= · =paladin-scope=, typology =mode-entity=) rewrite themselves on every run and are not rows.

* campaigns — 10-07, the quest proven on a rig before any =go=

/"everything we've been doing was purely theoretical. I want you to change that now. I want you to do two test passes … whenever you discover that you haven't designed something, you take a step back and you collect those, and then holistically, once you're done, I want you to do an integration and really design and think about how to implement this."/ — /"dont call it a testing sweep. call it a testing campaign"/

Two campaigns on a scratchpad copy of the repo (=scratchpad/rig=, =rig-a=: no =.git=, no =~/.viva=, =node_modules= symlinked, mikro 6.6.7 — the live driver; a v7 delta goes to m78). Campaign 1 — component level: a leg implements its milestone's tangle in the rig, writes the tests =* testing assessment= names, runs the suites, and COLLECTS what the quest left undesigned, never designs it; a leg is an Opus agent on =scratchpad/BRIEF.md=. The design pass (the Fable exception beef named, =scratchpad/BRIEF-design.md=) ruled every unknown and rewrote this quest. Campaign 2 — integration, Fable (/"also fable"/), =scratchpad/BRIEF-c2.md=: the legs merged onto one rig, the DESIGNED blocks landed and proven, the systems booted together, anima's and ghost's consumer side implemented and tested; the quest tangleable at its end.
